  [LMB]
  [RMB]
  [LMB]
  [Clipboard]
  We’re looking for motivated, outgoing individuals to join our growing Utility Warehouse team as Independent Partners.
  
  This is a flexible, self-employed opportunity where you’ll help households reduce their monthly bills by introducing them to Utility Warehouse’s award-winning services, including energy, broadband, mobile and selected insurance products. You’ll also have the opportunity to build your own team by introducing others to the business.
  
  Whether you’re looking for a side income or want to build a full-time business, you’ll receive the training, mentoring and support needed to succeed.
  
  What You’ll Do
  
  * Introduce households to Utility Warehouse services.
  * Help customers compare and switch their energy and insurance where suitable.
  * Build and maintain strong customer relationships.
  * Generate leads through networking, referrals and social media.
  * Recruit and mentor new Partners who want to grow their own business.
  * Deliver excellent customer service before and after sign-up.
  
  What We’re Looking For
  
  * Excellent communication and people skills.
  * Self-motivated with a positive attitude.
  * Comfortable speaking with new people.
  * Willing to learn and develop new skills.
  * Able to work independently and manage your own schedule.
  * Sales or customer service experience is helpful but not essential.
  
  What We Offer
  
  * Flexible working hours around your lifestyle.
  * Work remotely from anywhere in the UK.
  * Comprehensive training and ongoing mentorship.
  * Uncapped commission with performance-based bonuses.
  * Opportunity to earn recurring residual income from your customer base.
  * Career progression and leadership opportunities.
  * Supportive team environment with regular coaching.
  
  Ideal For
  
  * People looking for a second income.
  * Students and graduates.
  * Parents wanting flexible work.
  * Sales and customer service professionals.
  * Anyone who enjoys helping others save money while building their own business.
